### [1] [Pre-training, Reasoning, Benchmarking: X-ray Report Generation on CheXpert Plus Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08813)
*Xiao Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Dan Xu,Yuehang Li,Shiao Wang,Bo Jiang,Yaowei Wang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对X射线报告生成领域缺乏标准化基准的问题，在CheXpert Plus数据集上构建了包含主流模型和大语言模型的综合基准，并提出MambaXray-PRB框架，通过三阶段大模型预训练和多模态思维链推理提升报告生成性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线报告生成（RRG）对减轻临床诊断负担、缩短患者等待时间具有重要价值，但该领域面临标准化基准不足以及通用大模型领域适应不佳的瓶颈。特别是新发布的CheXpert Plus数据集缺乏配套的基线实现和评估结果，阻碍了标准化训练、定量评估和后续算法间的公平比较。

Method: 研究首先构建了一个覆盖主流X射线报告生成模型与大型语言模型的综合基准，为后续方法提供可靠的比较基础。其次，提出名为MambaXray-PRB的新框架，采用多阶段大模型预训练和多模态思维链推理，包含三个连续阶段：自监督自回归建模、X射线-报告对比学习、以及面向推理与报告生成的后训练优化。

Result: 在IU X-ray、MIMIC-CXR和CheXpert Plus三个数据集上的大量实验验证了MambaXray-PRB在放射学报告生成任务上的有效性。源代码已开源于GitHub。

Conclusion: 该工作通过建立标准化基准填补了CheXpert Plus缺乏基线评估的空白，并提出MambaXray-PRB框架，在提升报告生成性能的同时增强了模型可解释性，为X射线报告生成领域的研究提供了可靠参考。

Abstract: X-ray image-based Radiology Report Generation (RRG) constitutes a critical research direction within medical artificial intelligence, with great potential to alleviate clinicians' diagnostic workload and shorten patient waiting periods. Despite substantial advances over recent years, the field faces evident bottlenecks stemming from insufficient standardized benchmarks and inadequate domain adaptation of generic large models. Notably, the newly released CheXpert Plus dataset is provided without accompanying baseline implementations and evaluation results, which impedes standardized training, quantitative evaluation and fair comparison among follow-up algorithms. To mitigate this limitation, we establish a comprehensive benchmark encompassing prevailing X-ray report generation models and Large Language Models on CheXpert Plus. This benchmark delivers a reliable comparative foundation for upcoming methods and enables researchers to rapidly identify state-of-the-art approaches within this domain. Beyond benchmark construction, we rethink X-ray RRG under the paradigm of large models and propose a novel framework termed MambaXray-PRB. Our framework improves report generation performance and enhances model interpretability via multi-stage large-model pre-training and multi-modal Chain-of-Thought reasoning. The pipeline consists of three successive phases: self-supervised auto-regressive modeling, X-ray-report contrastive learning, and post-training optimization for reasoning and report generation. Extensive experiments on IU X-ray, MIMIC-CXR, and CheXpert Plus datasets validate the effectiveness of MambaXray-PRB for radiology report generation. The source code of this paper is available on https://github.com/Event-AHU/Medical_Image_Analysis

</details>


### [2] [Autonomous Driving Research Requires a Community-Driven Data Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08825)
*Jinsu Yoo,Zanming Huang,Katie Z Luo,Zheda Mai,Qiyuan Wu,Bharath Hariharan,Mark Campbell,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 自动驾驶虽已商业落地，但距离"随时随地、对任何人都可靠运行"的通用社会愿景仍很远。作者认为瓶颈不在建模，而在当前以少数基准数据集为核心的数据范式。尽管社区已在近50个国家产出600多个自动驾驶数据集，但多数因碎片化、可见度低、协议不兼容和基准激励而未被充分利用。文章主张建立协作式、社区驱动的数据范式，以促进数据发现、复用、整合与评估，降低参与门槛，并呼吁产学研合作将碎片化数据转化为共享社区基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶取得显著进展并已商业化，但仍无法实现"随时随地、对任何人都可靠运行"的通用社会承诺。作者认为这一差距的根源不是建模能力不足，而是数据范式的问题：研究过度依赖少数空间与场景覆盖有限的基准数据集，而社区积累的600多个、覆盖近50个国家的数据集大多处于低利用状态。造成低利用的原因是数据碎片化、可见度低、协议不兼容，以及基准激励机制将注意力集中在少数主导数据集上。因此需要一种新的数据范式来释放这些数据的价值。

Method: 这是一篇立场/观点性论文，而非提出具体算法。作者通过论证与分析，提出协作式、社区驱动的数据范式，并阐述其关键原则，同时展示一个早期实现案例，最后发出跨学术界与产业界协作的倡议，以将碎片化数据集转化为共享的社区基础设施，支撑"随时随地"的自动驾驶。

Result: 文章给出了对现状的诊断性结论：600多个数据集、覆盖近50个国家的丰富资源并未转化为广泛的研究影响，多数数据集因碎片化、可见度有限、协议不兼容和基准激励集中而严重未被充分利用。同时提出了协作式社区驱动数据范式，并给出其核心原则与一个早期实现的示例。

Conclusion: 作者认为，实现"随时随地、对任何人都可靠"的自动驾驶，关键在于转变数据范式，而非仅提升建模能力。应推动协作式、社区驱动的数据生态：改善多样数据集的发现、复用、整合与评估，让未被充分探索的数据更易获取、更有研究回报，并降低新贡献者的参与门槛。最终呼吁学术界与产业界共同协作，将碎片化数据集转变为共享的社区基础设施。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has made remarkable progress, with recent AI advances enabling commercial deployments that are reshaping urban mobility. Yet the field remains far from its universal social promise: autonomous systems that can operate robustly anywhere, anytime, for anyone. We posit that this gap is not merely a modeling problem, but a problem of the prevailing data paradigm. Current research relies heavily on a few benchmark datasets with limited spatial and scenario coverage, even though the community has collectively produced over 600 autonomous driving datasets across nearly 50 countries. However, this abundance has not translated into broad research impact: most datasets remain significantly underused due to fragmentation, limited visibility, incompatible protocols, and benchmark incentives that concentrate attention on a few dominant datasets. We therefore argue that autonomous driving research requires a collaborative, community-driven data paradigm. Such a paradigm would improve the discovery, reuse, integration, and evaluation of diverse datasets; make underexplored data easier and more rewarding to study; and lower the barrier for new contributors. We outline its key principles, illustrate an early realization, and call for collaboration across academia and industry to transform fragmented datasets into shared community infrastructure for anytime-anywhere autonomy.

</details>


### [3] [PanoPed: Beyond Bounding Boxes for Sim-to-Real Panoramic Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08826)
*Qinfeng Zhu,Weiguang Zhao,Yunxi Jiang,Anh Nguyen,Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景行人跟踪基准PanoPed与轻量角定位头Sextant，用角度测量思想提升球面行人跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景相机可全方位跟踪行人，但平面边界框无法完整描述行人在球面上的位置，缺少针对全方向的仿真到现实行人跟踪基准与方法。

Method: 构建PanoPed-S（108,000帧，含固定、四足、无人机相机，配同步掩码、深度、相机位姿与3D行人状态）和PanoPed-R（28,002真实帧，其中16,247密集标注）；提出Sextant——仅约0.035M参数、即插即用的角定位头，复用冻结检测器、保持跟踪身份、无需额外图像编码器。

Result: 在PanoPed-S测试中Sextant取得最佳，将最强基线MOTIP从47.30提升至49.49 HOTA，八个测试序列全部提升；合成训练的头在真实视频上无需微调即可将MOTIP和HAT提升0.96-1.14 HOTA，且两个随机种子在所有真实序列上均有改善；HAT+Sextant在未添加定位图像编码器的系统中表现最佳。

Conclusion: ERP矩形无法唯一确定可见行人的球面中心与角范围，而检测器的视觉查询仍含相关信息；基于六分仪角度测量思想的Sextant能以极低参数代价有效提升全景行人跟踪，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Full-sphere panoramic cameras let fixed monitoring systems and mobile robots track people in every direction, but a planar bounding box does not fully describe where a person is on the sphere. We introduce PanoPed, a sim-to-real benchmark for pedestrian tracking on the full sphere. PanoPed-S contains 108,000 frames from fixed, quadruped-mounted, and drone-mounted cameras, with synchronized masks, depth, camera poses, and 3D pedestrian states. PanoPed-R adds 28,002 real frames from fixed cameras, 16,247 of them densely annotated. We find that an ERP rectangle cannot uniquely determine the spherical center and angular extent of the visible person, while the detector's visual query still carries information about them. Inspired by the sextant's use of angular measurements to locate objects, we propose Sextant, a plug-and-play angular localization head with only about 0.035M parameters. It reuses a frozen detector, keeps track identities unchanged, and needs no extra image encoder. Sextant gives the best result in our PanoPed-S test comparison, raising the strongest baseline, MOTIP, from 47.30 to 49.49 HOTA, with gains on all eight test sequences. Without fine-tuning on real data, the same synthetic-trained heads improve MOTIP and HAT by 0.96-1.14 HOTA on real video, and both seeds improve every real sequence. HAT+Sextant scores best among the compared systems that add no localization image encoder.

</details>


### [4] [MoR-MLLM: Mixture of Recursions for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08830)
*Pengcheng Zheng,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaxin Yan,Sihan Cao,Jianwei Zhang,Xudong Wang,Jiaquan Zhang,Jewon Lee,Tae-Ho Kim,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoR-MLLM，基于Mixture-of-Recursions实现按token自适应递归深度的计算稀疏多模态大模型，降低训练内存与计算复杂度同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型推理能力强但计算与内存需求巨大，难以实际部署。现有轻量骨干方案仍因静态稀疏性和深度分配而计算密集，无法适应每个token的语义复杂度。

Method: 基于Mixture-of-Recursions框架，引入按token的自适应递归，动态调整递归深度，将更多计算分配给视觉或语言上困难的token，跳过简单token的冗余运算；设计三阶段MoR-Tuning策略和熵正则化损失以稳定多模态递归稀疏训练并促进路由分布多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，与近期先进的小型多模态大模型相比，MoR-MLLM大幅降低训练内存和计算复杂度，同时在多种视觉-语言任务上保持高性能。

Conclusion: MoR-MLLM通过自适应按token递归的计算稀疏机制，在保持多模态任务性能的同时显著降低训练成本，为高效多模态大模型部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning capabilities across vision and language tasks. However, their massive computational and memory demands hinder real-world deployment. While recent efforts reduce costs by employing lightweight language backbones, existing paradigms remain computation-dense due to their static sparsity and depth allocation, which cannot adapt to the semantic complexity of each token. To this end, we propose MoR-MLLM, a computation-sparse MLLM based on the recent Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR) framework. MoR-MLLM introduces adaptive per-token recursion, allowing the model to dynamically adjust its recursive depth and allocate more computation to visually or linguistically challenging tokens while skipping redundant operations for simpler ones. To stabilize the training of recursive sparsity in multimodal settings, we further design a three-stage MoR-Tuning strategy and an entropy-regularized loss to encourage diverse routing distributions. Extensive experiments show that compared with recent advanced tiny MLLMs, our proposed MoR-MLLM can greatly reduce the training memory and computation complexity while retaining high performance on various vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [5] [VCR-Bench: A Modular Open-Source Benchmark for Video Classification Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08936)
*Maksim Plinskiy,Aleksandr Gushchin,Sergey Lavrushkin,Dmitriy S. Vatolin,Anastasia Antsiferova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出VCR-Bench，一个模块化的开源视频分类鲁棒性评测基准框架，统一了视频加载、模型封装、对抗攻击与防御、感知指标、配置预设和结果日志，集成了30个视频分类模型、14种攻击和10种防御，并在Kinetics-400子集上进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分类鲁棒性已有多种基准，但视频领域缺乏对应基准。视频分类的时间维度为对抗攻击、防御和预处理引入了额外自由度，时间采样、扰动预算和指标聚合的相互作用在图像场景中没有直接对应物。这导致视频分类器鲁棒性的研究分散、实现互不兼容，报告的数字难以复现和分析。

Method: 作者提出VCR-Bench，一个模块化开源基准框架，标准化视频加载、分类器封装、对抗攻击与防御、感知指标、配置预设和结果日志。框架在统一评测协议下集成了30个视频分类模型、14种对抗攻击和10种防御封装。

Result: 在Kinetics-400子集上对代表性视频分类器、攻击和防御进行了评测，报告了干净准确率、攻击成功率、感知质量、运行时间和内存占用等指标。

Conclusion: VCR-Bench已公开发布，提供文档化安装、可复现的运行预设、组件扩展接口以及复现报告结果的脚本，代码地址为https://github.com/msu-video-group/vcr-bench，为视频分类鲁棒性研究提供了统一、可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Robustness of image classification has several benchmarks, but their video counterparts are absent. In video classification temporal dimension introduces additional degrees of freedom for adversarial attacks, defenses, and preprocessing. Temporal sampling, perturbation budgets, and metric aggregation also interact in ways with no direct analogue in the image setting. Therefore, robustness for video classifiers is studied across scattered, incompatible implementations, making reported numbers hard to reproduce and analyze. We introduce VCR-Bench, a modular open-source benchmark framework that standardizes video loading, wrappers for classifiers, adversarial attacks and defenses, perceptual metrics, configuration presets, and result logging. VCR-Bench currently integrates 30 video classification models, 14 adversarial attacks, and 10 defense wrappers under a common evaluation protocol. We evaluate representative video classifiers, attacks, and defenses on Kinetics-400 subset, reporting clean accuracy, attack success rate, perceptual quality, runtime, and memory usage. VCR-Bench is released with documented installation, reproducible run presets, component-extension interfaces, and scripts for reproducing the reported results at https://github.com/msu-video-group/vcr-bench.

</details>


### [6] [SPW-Nav: A Streaming Panoramic World Model for Language-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08941)
*Yunheng Liu,Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Minggui Teng,Jiaming Tan,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPW-Nav，一种流式全景世界模型，可理解自然语言移动指令，从单张全景图实时生成一分钟2K 360度视频，并支持指令的动态切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的全景视频生成可服务于交互式3D场景探索、虚拟现实体验和具身智能体训练等下游应用。但现有全景生成器只能沿预定义轨迹生成，而交互式世界模型则在透视视图中依赖低层动作进行交互，缺乏对自然语言移动指令的理解能力，也缺乏实时、长时流式生成能力。

Method: SPW-Nav将每条自然语言指令解读为上一帧全景图中的相机运动。其关键技术包括：球面旋转解耦（在球面上精确施加旋转）、姿态对齐条件（使平移输入在长流生成中保持有界）、以及结合少步生成器的多项记忆机制，从而在指令变化时持续生成场景。此外，研究还构建了SPW-NavSet数据集，包含带相机轨迹和经过验证指令的全景视频。

Result: 在语言驱动下，SPW-Nav在相机跟随准确度和视频质量方面均优于现有全景生成器，并支持运行过程中的即时指令切换。

Conclusion: SPW-Nav实现了语言引导的实时流式全景世界建模，兼顾长时生成稳定性与指令交互灵活性，为全景视频生成与交互式应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language-guided panoramic video generation benefits various downstream applications, such as interactive 3D scene exploration, virtual reality experiences, and embodied agent training. Existing panoramic generators follow predefined trajectories, and interactive world models act through low-level actions in perspective views. We propose SPW-Nav, a streaming panoramic world model that understands movement instructions and streams one minute of 2K 360-degree video in real time from a single panorama. SPW-Nav interprets each instruction in the previously generated panorama as camera motion. Spherical rotation decoupling applies rotation exactly on the sphere, pose-aligned conditioning keeps translation inputs bounded over long streams, and a multi-term memory with a few-step generator continues the scene as instructions change. We also build SPW-NavSet, panoramic videos with camera trajectories and verified instructions. Driven by language, SPW-Nav outperforms prior panoramic generators in camera-following accuracy and video quality, and supports on-the-fly instruction switching.

</details>


### [7] [One-Slide Calibration of Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08944)
*Ming Ren Hou,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlideRuler，利用切片自身区域作为内参，学习扫描仪间转换图，在保持病理基础模型冻结的情况下校准单次扫描的嵌入漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描仪差异会改变病理基础模型对同一组织的表征，导致跨成像系统使用冻结模型时嵌入不一致，需要一种无需改动模型即可校正采集引入漂移的方法。

Method: 以切片内区域作为内部对照，从配对重扫数据学习转换图，实现推理阶段基于单次扫描的校准，同时保持基础模型固定；并提出源锚定变体以减少源特征位移。

Result: 在两种编码器和五台SCORPION扫描仪上，学习到的转换使平均目标-源嵌入距离相比原始嵌入降低16.3-38.5%；与同扫描仪无关对照相比，四种评估设置（含扫描仪留出）均呈正的同切片贡献；源锚定变体使源特征位移降低47.7-83.6%，同时保留大部分对齐收益。

Conclusion: SlideRuler通过从切片自身提取校准信息，为跨成像系统一致使用冻结病理模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scanner variation changes how pathology foundation models represent the same tissue. We introduce SlideRuler, which uses regions within a slide as internal controls to estimate and correct acquisition-induced shifts in other regions. A transfer map learned from paired rescans enables calibration from a single scan at inference while keeping the foundation model fixed. Across two encoders and five SCORPION scanners, learned transfer reduces mean target-to-source embedding distance by 16.3-38.5% relative to raw embeddings. Comparisons with unrelated same-scanner controls reveal a positive same-slide contribution across all four evaluation settings, including scanner holdout. A source-anchored variant reduces source-feature displacement by 47.7-83.6% relative to learned transfer while retaining most of its alignment gain. By drawing calibration information from the slide itself, SlideRuler offers a path toward more consistent use of frozen pathology models across imaging systems.

</details>


### [8] [RACER: Reflective Agent Coupling Query Interpretation and Tool-Based Retrieval for Frame Selection in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08954)
*Yiyang Huang,Yitian Zhang,Yizhou Wang,Jianglin Lu,Qihua Dong,Hailing Wang,Huimin Zeng,Mingyuan Zhang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练反思式智能体框架RACER，将长视频帧选择分解为查询理解与证据定位两步，由轻量Vid-LLM重写子查询、嵌入模型做检索，形成反思循环，在多个基准上稳定提升长视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频帧选择需从大规模候选池中检索跨片段的相关帧，现有主流方法存在两类瓶颈：基于相似度的方法有"查询理解鸿沟"，基于判断的方法有"解释—选择鸿沟"。作者从任务分解视角出发，试图同时缓解这两大问题。

Method: RACER为免训练（training-free）的反思式智能体框架：轻量Vid-LLM仅负责将复杂查询改写为显式化隐式需求的子查询，缓解查询理解鸿沟；嵌入模型作为检索工具，依据子查询定位相关证据，使Vid-LLM免于直接选帧，缓解解释—选择鸿沟；检索到的帧再回馈给Vid-LLM精炼子查询，构成反思循环，迭代提升查询理解与帧选择。

Result: 在多个基准上，RACER持续提升长视频理解效果；即使使用能力有限的组件，也能实现有效的帧选择，说明智能体式集成能让这些组件增强能力更强的Vid-LLM。

Conclusion: 通过显式的任务分解与反思循环，RACER在不训练的前提下统一缓解了长视频帧选择的两大鸿沟，并证明智能体式集成可为强弱组件协同带来增益。

Abstract: Video large language models (Vid-LLMs) excel at diverse video-language tasks by reasoning over selected frames. However, frame selection for long videos remains challenging, as it requires retrieving relevant frames distributed across segments from a large candidate pool given complex queries. This paper investigates dominant approaches to long-video frame selection from a task-decomposition perspective, identifying two key challenges: the Query Comprehension Gap in similarity-based methods and the Interpretation--Selection Gap in judgment-based methods. To address them, we propose RACER, a training-free reflective agentic framework that decomposes long-video frame selection into query interpretation driven by a lightweight Vid-LLM and evidence localization supported by an embedding model serving as a retrieval tool. Specifically, the Vid-LLM is responsible solely for reformulating the complex query into sub-queries that make implicit information requirements explicit, mitigating the Query Comprehension Gap. Meanwhile, the retrieval tool leverages these sub-queries to localize relevant evidence, relieving the Vid-LLM of direct frame selection and thus addressing the Interpretation--Selection Gap. Finally, the retrieved frames are fed back to the Vid-LLM for sub-query refinement, forming a reflection loop that iteratively improves query interpretation and frame selection. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that RACER consistently improves long video understanding. Notably, RACER achieves effective frame selection even with limited-capability components, demonstrating that agentic integration enables these components to enhance more capable Vid-LLMs.

</details>


### [9] [S2Tok: Streaming 3D Gaussian Reconstruction with Persistent Spatial Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08978)
*Fang Li,Jiraphon Yenphraphai,Quentin Herau,Depu Meng,Yihan Hu,Tianshuo Xu,Narendra Ahuja,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2Tok是一个前馈框架，从无标定图像流中维护尺寸自适应、持久的场景状态，通过选择性扩展实现流式3D重建与渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建需要持久场景状态以整合新证据并保持可渲染性，而潜空间token如何在线维护（兼顾已知区域更新与新内容扩展）尚待解决。

Method: 利用空间感知transformer将新观测与持久场景token整合，学习型准入模块选择性地扩展表示以限制冗余存储，并用层次化解码器与高斯头将演化的状态转为非像素对齐的3D高斯。

Result: 在四个基准上以紧凑的高斯表示实现了有竞争力的流式渲染质量。

Conclusion: 结果支持将潜空间token作为在线3D重建的持久计算状态，结合学习式场景更新与显式高斯渲染。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction requires more than a sequence of geometric predictions: it requires a persistent scene state that can incorporate new evidence and remain renderable as observations arrive. Latent spatial tokens offer a promising representation for this purpose, but constructing them from an image collection leaves open how to maintain them online, where each observation may both revisit known regions and reveal new content. We introduce S2Tok, a feed-forward framework that maintains a size-adaptive, persistent scene state from uncalibrated image streams. Its central idea is to distinguish updates to the existing representation from selective expansion. A spatially informed transformer integrates each incoming observation with the persistent scene tokens, while a learned admission module selectively expands the representation to limit redundant storage. A hierarchical decoder and Gaussian head convert the evolving state into non-pixel-aligned 3D Gaussians, enabling novel-view rendering without caching previous frames. Experiments across four benchmarks demonstrate competitive streaming rendering quality with compact Gaussian representations. These results support latent spatial tokens as a persistent computational state for online 3D reconstruction, combining learned scene updates with explicit Gaussian rendering.

</details>


### [10] [Zero-Shot Brain MRI Inpainting with 2.5D Unconditional Flow Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08983)
*Arnela Hadzic,Franz Thaler,Simon Johannes Joham,Martin Urschler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于2.5D无条件流先验的零样本脑MRI修复框架，通过流匹配学习相邻轴位切片三元组分布，结合Restora-Flow求解器实现掩码约束修复，在BraTS 2026验证集上取得SSIM 0.816等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI体积修复对下游任务（配准、脑提取、分割）至关重要，但3D方法计算开销过大，2D逐片方法存在严重的层间不连续。此外传统模型依赖条件训练，需针对特定任务学习掩码输入，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出利用2.5D无条件流先验的零样本修复框架，在训练时学习相邻轴位切片三元组的联合分布以捕捉上下方向的空间上下文，并在损失中显式排除病理区域。推理时沿深度轴自回归处理三元组，采用Restora-Flow求解器以输入掩码约束无条件先验。最后对多个随机重建结果进行集成平均。

Result: 在BraTS 2026修复挑战官方验证集上，SSIM为0.816±0.112，MSE为0.007±0.005，PSNR为22.923±4.343。2.5D策略解决了2D基线的结构不连续问题，在轴位、矢状位和冠状位均保持体积一致性。

Conclusion: 2.5D无条件流先验结合掩码约束求解器，可在无需全3D卷积开销和条件训练的情况下实现准确的零样本脑MRI修复，兼顾计算效率与体积一致性。

Abstract: Generative inpainting of brain MRI volumes is essential for synthesizing healthy tissue in pathological regions, improving the accuracy and reliability of automated downstream brain analysis applications such as image registration, brain extraction, and segmentation. However, standard 3D approaches are computationally prohibitive, while efficient 2D slice-wise methods suffer from severe inter-slice discontinuities. Furthermore, traditional models rely on conditional training, requiring task-specific learning of masked inputs. We propose a zero-shot brain MRI inpainting framework utilizing 2.5D unconditional flow priors to capture spatial context along the superior-inferior axis without the overhead of full 3D convolutions. During training, our flow matching model learns the joint distribution of adjacent axial slice triplets, modeling the manifold of healthy brain anatomy while explicitly excluding pathological regions from the loss function. At inference, the model processes the input triplets autoregressively along the depth axis. We employ the Restora-Flow solver to constrain the unconditional prior using the input mask, achieving accurate zero-shot inpainting. Evaluations show our 2.5D strategy resolves the structural discontinuities of 2D baselines, synthesizing plausible healthy tissue while maintaining volumetric consistency across the axial, sagittal, and coronal planes. As a final step, we generate and average an ensemble of multiple stochastic reconstructions to form the final prediction. Quantitative results benchmarked on the official BraTS 2026 Inpainting Challenge validation set demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach, yielding an SSIM of 0.816 $\pm$ 0.112, MSE of 0.007 $\pm$ 0.005, and PSNR of 22.923 $\pm$ 4.343. Code is available at https://github.com/imigraz/brats2026-inpainting.

</details>


### [11] [Personalize at Test Time: Learning User Preferences for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09015)
*Jiamu Bai,Jiaming Hu,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种直接从用户历史图像偏好对中学习个性化奖励模型的方法，通过自编码器压缩视觉属性并用低维权重向量建模用户偏好，实现高效个性化图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量图像，但难以对齐个体用户偏好。现有方法依赖人工标注或视觉语言模型提取偏好信息，带来高昂的标注或计算成本，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 首先用自编码器将数百个视觉属性压缩为50个属性锚定的偏好维度，并训练评估器沿这些维度为图像打分；然后将每个用户的偏好表示为共享维度分数的线性组合，在Bradley-Terry模型下通过最大化其观测成对偏好的似然来估计用户特定权重；最后用学到的个性化奖励在推理时引导图像生成，同时保持扩散模型冻结。

Result: 在真实用户偏好数据上，该方法取得约77%的留出成对偏好预测准确率，并提升了生成图像与个体用户偏好的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 通过将个性化建模转化为低维权重向量的优化，该方法简化了优化过程，实现了从稀疏反馈中高效、数据节约的个性化，并能有效引导扩散模型生成符合用户偏好的图像。

Abstract: Diffusion models can generate high-quality images, yet aligning their outputs with individual user preferences remains challenging. A key bottleneck is accurately modeling diverse user preferences from limited feedback. Existing approaches often rely on labor-intensive manual preference annotations or vision-language models (VLM) to extract preference information from user interaction histories, introducing substantial annotation or computational costs that limit scalability. We propose an approach that learns personalized reward models directly from users' historical image preference pairs. First, we use an autoencoder to compress hundreds of visual attributes into 50 attribute-anchored preference dimensions and train an evaluator to score images along these dimensions. We then represent each user's preferences as a linear combination of the shared dimension scores, estimating the user-specific weights by maximizing the likelihood of their observed pairwise preferences under the Bradley-Terry model. This formulation reduces per-user adaptation to optimizing a low-dimensional weight vector, simplifying optimization and enabling data-efficient personalization from sparse feedback. The learned personalized rewards guide image generation at inference time while keeping the diffusion model frozen. Experiments on real-user preference data show that our approach achieves approximately 77% held-out pairwise preference prediction accuracy and improves the alignment of generated images with individual user preferences.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Explanation: Debugging Medical Imaging Models via Concept Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09031)
*Samrajya Thapa,Daniel J. Quest,Timothy L. Kline,Carrie L. Langstraat,Emanuel C. Trabuco,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个即插即用的基于概念的医学影像可解释性框架，通过将单模态编码器对齐到BioMedCLIP构建概念瓶颈模型（CBM），实现概念级干预，用于因果与虚假相关概念区分、专家验证及反事实样本微调，并在Mayo超声和CheXpert数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像模型常为黑箱，限制了可解释性与系统性调试，如何实现可控、可解释的临床深度学习模型改进是核心动机。

Method: 将单模态编码器对齐到BioMedCLIP，构建概念瓶颈模型（CBM）以支持概念级干预；干预用于隔离因果概念与虚假相关概念，结合领域专家验证，并生成反事实样本进行定向微调。

Result: 在Mayo Clinic超声数据集和CheXpert 5x200胸片数据集上评估，概念干预实现了可靠的模型诊断，同时通过引导微调保持了预测性能，有时甚至有所提升。

Conclusion: 该框架对临床深度学习模型的可控、可解释改进具有实用价值。

Abstract: Medical imaging models often operate as black boxes, limiting interpretability and systematic debugging. We introduce an easy-to-use, plug-and-play framework for concept-based interpretation and model refinement. By aligning a single-modality encoder to BioMedCLIP, we construct a Concept Bottleneck Model (CBM) that enables concept-level interventions. These interventions allow us to isolate causal versus spuriously correlated concepts, validate insights with domain experts, and generate counterfactual samples for targeted fine-tuning. We evaluate our framework on a Mayo Clinic ultrasound dataset and the CheXpert 5x200 chest X-ray dataset. Results demonstrate that concept intervention enables reliable model diagnosis while maintaining, and occasionally improving predictive performance via guided fine-tuning. Our findings highlight the practical value of this framework for controlled, interpretable refinement of clinical deep learning models.

</details>


### [13] [Shape-Bayes: Bayesian Inference of Structured Shapes under Visual Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09032)
*Mani Kumar Tellamekala,Tosh Brown,Michel Valstar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shape-Bayes，一个将不确定性感知的视觉感知与贝叶斯形状推理结合的概率框架，用于解决遮挡条件下确定性形状回归的结构崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从像素推断结构化形状（如人脸）本质上具有歧义性，但现有方法多将其视为确定性回归任务，预测固定空间坐标。作者发现，当视觉证据模糊或不完整（如严重遮挡）时，确定性回归表现脆弱，会出现结构崩溃，预测出不连贯的形状或退化为通用平均形状。

Method: Shape-Bayes 动态地在视觉证据与几何先验之间加权，推断结构有效的形状后验，而非强制点估计。其包含三部分：(1) 基础模型预测带噪声的关键点及蒸馏的偶然不确定性；(2) 轻量 Transformer 将这些观测编码为 PCA 形状流形上的自适应先验；(3) 可微贝叶斯求解器，通过平衡噪声预测与该先验计算闭式后验。在人脸形状回归这一包含复杂非刚性变形和严格解剖约束的测试平台上进行验证。

Result: Shape-Bayes 保证完整的结构完整性，相比最先进确定性模型在 IDR 上取得最高约 34% 的绝对提升；同时产生高度校准的不确定性边界，并将相对误差最多降低 12.5%，在严重遮挡下的鲁棒 2D 人脸形状回归中达到新的最优水平。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性感知感知与贝叶斯形状推理结合，Shape-Bayes 有效克服了确定性回归在歧义视觉条件下的结构崩溃问题，实现了结构完整性、校准不确定性与精度的共同提升，为鲁棒 2D 人脸形状回归树立了新标杆。

Abstract: Perceiving structured shapes, such as human faces, from pixels is an inherently ambiguous task in real-world conditions. Yet, shape inference is largely posed as a deterministic regression task predicting fixed spatial coordinates. We find that deterministic regression is brittle when visual evidence is ambiguous or incomplete; under severe occlusions deterministic models exhibit structural collapse, predicting incoherent shapes or reverting to generic averages. To address this, we introduce Shape-Bayes, a probabilistic framework that couples uncertainty-aware visual perception with Bayesian shape reasoning. Rather than forcing point estimates, Shape-Bayes dynamically weights visual evidence against geometric priors to infer a structurally valid shape posterior. Demonstrated on human face shape regression, a rigorous testbed featuring complex non-rigid deformations and strict anatomical constraints, Shape-Bayes comprises: (1) a base model predicting noisy landmarks alongside distilled aleatoric uncertainties; (2) a lightweight Transformer encoding these observations into an adaptive prior over a PCA shape manifold; and (3) a differentiable Bayesian solver computing closed-form posteriors by balancing the noisy predictions against this prior. By guaranteeing complete structural integrity, Shape-Bayes achieves an absolute improvement of up to ~34% IDR over state-of-the-art deterministic models. Simultaneously, it yields highly calibrated uncertainty bounds and reduces relative error by up to 12.5%, establishing a new state-of-the-art for robust 2D face shape regression under severe occlusion. The project page is at https://shape-bayes.github.io.

</details>


### [14] [GUARD: Geometric Uncertainty-Aware Point Cloud Denoising and Segmentation for Robotic Hard Disk Drive Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09068)
*Zuoxu Wang,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUARD是一个几何不确定性感知框架，在单次前向传播中完成点云滤波与分割，通过区分几何可靠性与语义置信度来提升机器人拆解中的部件识别可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人拆解需要部件级表示，但硬盘点云中扫描与重建产生的结构化幽灵伪影在局部上类似有效部件，语义预测置信度无法判断底层几何是否可靠，这构成工程信息问题。

Method: GUARD将多尺度几何Transformer与多带宽随机傅里叶特征高斯过程结合，估计逐点几何不确定性，并辅以预测熵，在前向传播中同时抑制不可靠测量并保留有效结构。

Result: 在2,745个真实HDD点云上，PointNet++分割mIoU从0.7739提升至0.8318；在ShapeNetPart和ScanNet上验证了跨损坏类型、点云域和分割主干的鲁棒性；手工标注ScanNet上几何不确定性检测损坏点的F1为0.7931，远高于预测熵的0.2212。

Conclusion: 结果表明区分几何可靠性与语义置信度具有重要价值，并揭示了伪影抑制与有效结构保留之间的权衡，为部件识别及后续机器人操作提供了可靠性感知的3D测量解读方法。

Abstract: Reliable robotic disassembly requires part-level representations that distinguish genuine component geometry from scanning and reconstruction artifacts. In point clouds of hard disk drives (HDDs), structured ghost artifacts can resemble valid components locally while remaining inconsistent with the overall geometry, allowing erroneous measurements to receive plausible semantic labels. This creates an engineering information problem: semantic prediction confidence alone does not establish whether the underlying geometry is reliable. We propose \textbf{GUARD}, a geometric uncertainty-aware framework that performs point filtering and segmentation within a single forward pass by modeling the reliability of learned geometric representations. GUARD combines a multi-scale geometric transformer with a multi-bandwidth random Fourier feature Gaussian Process to estimate per-point geometric uncertainty, complemented by predictive entropy to suppress unreliable measurements while preserving informative structures. Evaluation on 2,745 real HDD point clouds shows that GUARD improves PointNet++ segmentation mean intersection over union from 0.7739 to 0.8318. Additional experiments on ShapeNetPart and ScanNet examine robustness across corruption types, point-cloud domains, and segmentation backbones. On manually annotated ScanNet samples, geometric uncertainty achieves a corrupted-point detection F1 score of 0.7931, compared with 0.2212 for predictive entropy. The results demonstrate the value of distinguishing geometric reliability from semantic confidence and reveal a tradeoff between artifact suppression and preservation of informative structures. GUARD contributes a reliability-aware approach to interpreting imperfect 3D measurements for component identification and subsequent robotic handling. Project website: https://001-wang.github.io/GUARD_Point_denoiser/.

</details>


### [15] [OverLay++: Dense-Overlap Layout-to-Image Generation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09071)
*Shivansh Aggarwal,Shresth Grover,Divyansh Srivastava,Haiyang Xu,Bingnan Li,Xiang Zhang,Ethan J. Armand,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模布局到图像数据集OverLay++，包含约50万张图像、平均每张6.6个物体，标注密度为现有数据集的1.67倍，物体描述长度超过现有6倍，显著提升复杂场景下的可控图像生成效果与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局到图像生成方法在包含大量重叠与交互物体的复杂场景中表现不佳，作者认为训练数据是关键瓶颈：已有数据集缺乏密集、复杂物体交互的样本，因此需要构建一个结构复杂、标注密集且语义丰富的新数据集。

Method: 构建OverLay++数据集：通过一个简单且可扩展的数据生成流程，产出约50万张图像，平均每张含6.6个物体，并提供密集且相互重叠的物体标注以及更长的逐物体描述（文本长度约为现有数据集的6倍）。随后在多个基准上用该数据集训练当前最先进的布局到图像生成方法进行验证。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，使用OverLay++训练的先进布局到图像方法均取得一致性提升，且收敛速度更快。

Conclusion: 密集、具备重叠感知以及描述丰富的监督信号对可控图像生成至关重要；OverLay++验证了数据质量与密度是提升复杂场景布局到图像生成能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Layout-to-Image generation has made substantial progress in spatial and object-level control. However, existing methods still struggle with complex scenes containing many overlapping and interacting objects. We argue that training data is a particular bottleneck: existing datasets lack examples with dense, complex object interactions. To address this gap, we introduce OverLay++, a large-scale Layout-to-Image dataset with structurally complex scenes. OverLay++ contains approximately 500K images with an average of 6.6 objects per image, exceeding existing datasets by 1.67 times in annotation density. Beyond annotation density, OverLay++ provides rich semantic detail with object captions over six times longer than in current datasets. Our dataset generation pipeline is simple and produces dense, overlapping object annotations with rich per-object captions. Across multiple benchmarks, state-of-the-art Layout-to-Image methods trained on the OverLay++ dataset show consistent improvement and faster convergence, demonstrating the importance of dense, overlap-aware, and caption-rich supervision for controllable image generation.

</details>


### [16] [DISRQAD: Diffusion Image Super-Resolution Quality Assessment Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09077)
*Nikita Kukuzei,Artem Borisov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Khaled Abud,Egor Chistov,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DISRQAD，一个面向扩散式图像超分辨率的主观质量数据集与诊断基准，包含1.4万张SR输出的MOS评分，发现现有质量指标在扩散输出上与主观评分一致性显著偏弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式图像超分辨率能生成低分辨率输入并不支持的、看似合理的细节，这使传统保真度导向的质量评估失效，而现有指标在这类输出上的表现缺乏系统性的主观质量基准检验。

Method: 构建DISRQAD数据集：对来自10种扩散方法和4种非扩散方法的14,000个SR输出收集MOS，覆盖4种低分辨率退化条件和x2/x4放大倍率；并评测51种标准全参考/无参考指标配置及11种改进变体，另以剪枝蒸馏的Q-ReAlign-mini学生模型作为基准使用案例。

Result: 在扩散输出上指标与MOS一致性明显更弱：最强标准无参考基线在扩散SR上SRCC仅0.431，而在非扩散SR上达0.813；Q-ReAlign-mini学生模型在扩散SR上达到0.496 SRCC。

Conclusion: DISRQAD衡量感知输出质量而非对输入的忠实度，支持跨生成器族与输入条件的指标行为分析，揭示了扩散式SR质量评估的显著空白，为开发对扩散特有伪影敏感的质量模型提供基础。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image super-resolution (SR) can create visually plausible detail that is not supported by the low-resolution input. We introduce DISRQAD, a subjective-quality dataset and diagnostic benchmark for this setting. It contains mean opinion scores (MOS) for 14,000 SR outputs from ten diffusion and four non-diffusion methods, spanning four low-resolution degradation conditions and x2/x4 upscaling. We evaluate 51 standard full-reference and no-reference metric configurations and 11 adapted variants. Agreement with MOS is substantially weaker on diffusion outputs: the strongest standard no-reference baseline reaches 0.431 SRCC on diffusion SR versus 0.813 on non-diffusion SR. As a case study in benchmark use, a pruned and distilled Q-ReAlign-mini student reaches 0.496 SRCC on diffusion SR. DISRQAD measures perceived output quality, not faithfulness to the input; it enables analysis of metric behavior across generator families and input conditions. Our findings reveal a substantial gap in the assessment of diffusion-based SR and provide a basis for developing quality models sensitive to diffusion-specific artifacts.

</details>


### [17] [Anaximander: Interactively Running Geospatial Deep Learning Models on Any Compute Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09085)
*Satej S. Soman,Akram Zaytar,Girmaw A. Tadesse,Gilles Q. Hacheme,Muhammad S. Danish,Inbal Becker-Reshef,Rahul Dodhia,Juan Lavista Ferres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Anaximander（nxmndr），一个开源系统，将多源深度学习模型与多种计算后端统一到一个交互式界面中，通过推理服务器与 QGIS 插件配合，实现卫星影像模型在 GIS 中的免代码部署、分块推理与地理配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GIS 中应用深度学习模型处理卫星影像对遥感从业者而言摩擦很大：模型格式不兼容，且面向不同计算环境（本地工作站到无服务器云服务）。每次评估都需要定制的部署、分块和地理配准代码，模型预测才能进入分析师的地图，这阻碍了系统性比较，而模型选择会直接影响农田制图、作物监测、灾害响应等业务结果。

Method: 系统后端是一个推理服务器，可从多种常用来源加载模型，并在任意可访问的计算后端上提供服务，支持会话管理与模型缓存，按分块流式返回结果。后端与一个 QGIS 插件配对，负责分块、结果重组、地理配准和实时的逐块状态可视化。另有一条用户界面路径可将图层图例作为提示注入视觉语言模型。

Result: 在农田田块制图任务上，对三个异构模型进行了免代码的并排比较：通过云 API 调用的 gpt-image-1、在远程 GPU 上运行的 Segment Anything Model 3（SAM3），以及在本机 CPU 上运行的 DelineateAnything。

Conclusion: 推理后端与协议均已开源，地址为 https://github.com/microsoft/nxmndr，为遥感从业者提供了统一的模型来源与计算位置选择方案。

Abstract: Applying deep learning models to satellite imagery from within geographic information systems (GIS) remains high-friction for remote sensing practitioners. Models arrive in incompatible formats and target different compute environments, from local workstations to serverless cloud services. As a result, every evaluation demands custom deployment, tiling, and georeferencing code before a single prediction reaches the analyst's map. This friction discourages systematic comparison in a domain where model choice directly affects operational outcomes such as field delineation, crop monitoring, and disaster response. We present Anaximander, an open-source system that unifies model source and compute location choice behind one interactive interface. The system's backend is an inference server that loads models from multiple commonly-used sources and serves them on any accessible compute backend. The server provides session management and model caching, and streams results back per tile. The backend is paired with a QGIS plugin that drives tiling, result reassembly, georeferencing, and real-time per-tile status visualization. An additional user-interface path injects layer legends as prompts into vision-language models. We demonstrate the system in a code-free side-by-side comparison of three heterogeneous models on an agricultural field delineation task: gpt-image-1 via a cloud API, Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) on a remote GPU, and DelineateAnything on a local CPU. The inference backend and protocol are open-source and available at https://github.com/microsoft/nxmndr.

</details>


### [18] [SPLATIFY: Reproduce, Discover, Innovate! From Papers and Ideas to Trainable 3DGS Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09116)
*Seemandhar Jain,Keshav Gupta,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPLATIFY是一个多智能体框架，可将3DGS论文自动转换为基于gsplat的可训练实现，通过五项创新在无公开代码的论文上达到专家级效果，并将开发时间从数周缩短至数分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）研究快速增长，研究者需耗费大量精力重新实现论文才能在其基础上进一步工作，而通用论文转代码方法与前沿模型在此任务上均告失败。

Method: 提出五项创新：(1) 面向gsplat的无上下文语法，基于模块化模板并为损失、致密化、渲染与优化设置扩展点，使生成代码天然满足架构不变量；(2) 忠实复现的架构要素，包括fork感知引用恢复、按拓扑依赖顺序的思维图合成、基于RAG的示例选择（来自20多个验证实现）以及视觉反馈（PSNR引导重生成、高斯级结构检查、VLM驱动修补）；(3) 知识驱动的组合式改进；(4) 跨学科方法发现；(5) SPLATIFY-Bench评估框架（30篇论文）。

Result: 在无公开代码的论文上，SPLATIFY匹配专家实现，将开发时间从数周降至数分钟，并通过组合式发现将PSNR最多提升2.4 dB；还合成了体素星云渲染等新方法。

Conclusion: SPLATIFY有效实现了3DGS论文的自动复现与改进，并展现出跨学科方法发现能力，可生成全新方法。

Abstract: The rapid growth of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) research demands significant effort to reimplement papers before building on them. We introduce SPLATIFY, a multi-agent framework that converts 3DGS papers into trainable gsplat-based implementations, where generic paper-to-code methods and frontier models fail. SPLATIFY achieves this through five innovations: (1) A context-free grammar for gsplat over a modular method template with extension points for losses, densification, rendering, and optimization, constraining synthesis so generated code satisfies gsplat's architectural invariants by construction. (2) Architectural elements for faithful reproduction: fork-aware citation recovery retrieving component-level code at function-level granularity, Graph-of-Thought synthesis in topological dependency order, RAG-guided in-context example selection from over 20 verified implementations, and visual feedback combining PSNR-guided regeneration, Gaussian-level structural checks, and VLM-driven patching. (3) Knowledge-driven compositional improvement that autonomously finds weaknesses and composes complementary regularizers, losses, and densification strategies to improve upon original results. (4) Interdisciplinary method discovery where agents retrieve physical priors from outside the 3DGS literature and compose them with rendering knowledge to produce methods for previously unaddressed scene types. (5) SPLATIFY-Bench, an evaluation framework across 30 diverse 3DGS papers. On papers without public code, SPLATIFY matches expert implementations while reducing development time from weeks to minutes, and through compositional discovery further improves PSNR by up to 2.4 dB. We additionally demonstrate novel methods for volumetric nebula rendering and other scientific domains, synthesized entirely by SPLATIFY.

</details>


### [19] [TopoCurve: Geometry-Aware Topology Reasoning via Bézier Curves in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09118)
*Mihai Bogdan Deaconu,Laura Dioşan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoCurve，一种基于端点固定三次贝塞尔曲线参数化车道表示的几何驱动3D拓扑推理架构，在OpenLane-V2基准上取得50.6 OLS的端到端纯相机方法最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑推理方法将车道建模为离散折线，缺乏平滑性、解析切方向和用于注意力的全局空间支撑，同时拓扑监督稀疏。作者希望借助统一的曲线几何表示来同时改善几何质量与拓扑推理。

Method: 将车道建模为端点固定的三次贝塞尔曲线，实现连续几何、精确端点与解析方向性。全流程共享该曲线几何：用多尺度傅里叶特征编码端点距离与切线对齐并注入拓扑头；采样曲线点作为几何对齐参考用于跨整条车道的可变形交叉注意力；并行曲线锚定注意力分支为"一对多"拓扑监督提供多样预测。

Result: 在OpenLane-V2基准上，无需任何后处理即达50.6 OLS，在端到端纯相机方法中刷新SOTA；端点检测DET_p达56.8，优于所有现有方法的52.6。

Conclusion: 曲线参数化表示可紧密耦合表征、特征聚合与监督，形成级联受益架构，验证了几何驱动设计在3D车道拓扑推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Topology reasoning jointly detects 3D lanes and traffic elements from multi-view images and infers their structural connectivity. Current methods model lanes as discrete polylines, lacking smoothness, analytical tangent directions, and global spatial support for attention, while providing sparse topology supervision. We propose TopoCurve, a geometry-driven architecture for 3D topology reasoning grounded in a structured parametric lane representation. Lanes are modeled as endpoint-fixed cubic Bézier curves, enabling continuous geometry with exact endpoints and analytically defined directionality. We exploit this shared curve geometry across the entire pipeline. Endpoint distance and tangent alignment are encoded with multi-scale Fourier features and injected into the topology head. Sampled curve points serve as geometry-aligned references for deformable cross-attention spanning the full lane. Parallel curve-anchored attention branches provide diverse predictions for one-to-many topology supervision. These components form a tightly coupled cascade where representation enables geometric reasoning, guides feature aggregation, and supports denser supervision. TopoCurve achieves 50.6 OLS on the OpenLane-V2 benchmark without any post-processing, establishing a new state-of-the-art among end-to-end camera-only methods, and outperforms all existing approaches on endpoint detection (56.8 vs. 52.6 on DET_p).

</details>


### [20] [Depth-to-RGB: Repurposing a Frozen Depth Estimator for Geometry-Guided Compositing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09125)
*Sanghyun Jo,Chae Yeon Lim,Donghwan Lee,Sihyun Kim,Soo Ye Kim,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Depth-to-RGB (D2R) 框架，为参考图像引导的对象合成任务预测尚未在 RGB 输入中观察到的合成场景深度，并在几何与光度质量上超越多个开源和闭源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考图像引导的对象合成方法仅依赖背景图、参考图和 2D 合成掩码，这些输入虽能引导外观与位置，但所生成场景的几何信息是隐式的，容易导致对象结构扭曲或周围环境改变。因此需要显式地为合成场景预测几何（深度）以提升合成质量。

Method: D2R 利用冻结的深度估计器，将配对完成场景的编码器特征作为目标，学习参考条件化的深度校正。校正后的表示由未改动的解码器映射为目标场景深度，并在 RGB 合成阶段由一个单独训练且保持固定的渲染器使用该深度。训练中采用编码器特征监督而非解码深度监督。此外提出 AnyInsertion++ 数据集，包含配对分布内与类别不相交两种划分以评估泛化能力。

Result: 在相同架构与训练条件下，编码器特征监督相比解码深度监督将 OOD Stage-1 AbsRel 降低 31.4%。完整 D2R 系统在两种配对划分上的估计器几何与光度质量均领先 12 个开源和 3 个闭源基线；在类别不相交数据上 AbsRel 降低 43.7%，PSNR 相比匹配的 RGB 基线提升 2.4 dB。在三个非配对基准上，D2R 在身份指标上领先，并将平均 CLIP 参考余弦距离相比最强基线降低 55%。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测合成场景深度并对冻结深度估计器进行参考条件化校正，D2R 显著提升了对象合成的几何保真度与光度质量，并在分布外与类别不相交场景下展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reference-based object compositing inserts or replaces an object using a background image, a reference image, and a 2D compositing mask. These inputs guide appearance and placement but leave the completed scene's geometry implicit, which can distort object structure or alter the surroundings. Our Depth-to-RGB (D2R) framework predicts composite depth for a scene not yet observed in the RGB inputs. It learns reference-conditioned corrections to a frozen depth estimator using encoder features of paired completed scenes as targets. The unchanged decoder maps the corrected representation to the intended scene's depth, which a separately trained renderer holds fixed during RGB synthesis. Under matched architecture and training, encoder-feature supervision reduces OOD Stage-1 AbsRel by 31.4% relative to decoded-depth supervision. We also introduce AnyInsertion++ with paired in-distribution and category-disjoint splits to evaluate generalization beyond compositing training categories. The complete D2R system leads 12 open-source and 3 closed-source baselines in estimator-derived geometry and photometric quality on both paired splits. On category-disjoint data, D2R reduces AbsRel by 43.7% and improves PSNR by 2.4 dB over the matched RGB baseline. Across three unpaired benchmarks, D2R leads both identity metrics and reduces mean CLIP reference cosine distance by 55% relative to the strongest baseline. Project page: https://shjo-april.github.io/Depth2RGB/

</details>


### [21] [RDGSplat: Render-Dedicated Geometry for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09173)
*Zhijie Zheng,Xinhao Xiang,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RDGSplat框架，从冻结的3D基础模型中解码出专用于渲染的第二个几何表示，在不破坏原度量预测的前提下提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型虽能通过挂载高斯头实现高效新视角合成，但其渲染视图质量不及所恢复的几何，因为几何是在度量目标下估计的，从未以渲染效果为评分标准。近期方法通过更新骨干网络权重来缓解，但这会丢弃模型原本的度量预测能力，且每换一个骨干就需重训。

Method: 提出Render-Dedicated Geometry Decoding：复制预训练解码器，仅以光度监督优化副本；并引入Target-Pose Conditioned Adapter，将解码器所读取的表征改为以目标相机位姿（而非目标图像）为条件进行重构。整个过程中预训练权重保持冻结。

Result: 在四个基准、三种前馈骨干上均提升新视角合成效果，且所有预训练权重冻结。在RE10K上，WM2.0从20.918 dB提升至24.266 dB，新增训练参数205.5M，而冻结骨干为1.4B，且该模型预测的深度与位姿保持不变。

Conclusion: 通过为渲染单独解码几何并保持骨干冻结，RDGSplat在不牺牲原度量预测（深度、位姿）的前提下显著提升渲染质量，且无需针对不同骨干重复训练。

Abstract: 3D foundation models enable efficient novel view synthesis by carrying a Gaussian head on the representation they already use for reconstruction. However, the views they render fall short of the geometry they recover, because that geometry is estimated under a metric objective and never scored on how it renders. Recent methods alleviate this by updating the backbone weights, but they thereby discard the metric predictions the model was built for and must be repeated for every new backbone. To this end, we propose RDGSplat, a framework that decodes a second geometry dedicated to rendering from a frozen 3D foundation model, leaving its metric predictions intact. In particular, we devise Render-Dedicated Geometry Decoding, which duplicates the pretrained decoders and optimizes the duplicates under photometric supervision alone. Then, a Target-Pose Conditioned Adapter is introduced to reformulate the representation those decoders read, conditioned on the target camera pose rather than the target image. Extensive experiments show that RDGSplat improves novel view synthesis across three feed-forward backbones on four benchmarks, with every pretrained weight frozen. On RE10K, it raises WM2.0 from 20.918 to 24.266\,dB while training 205.5\,M added parameters against a frozen 1.4\,B backbone, and the depth and pose the same model predicts are unchanged.

</details>


### [22] [One Frame, Full Heartbeat: ECG-Free 4D Cardiac Cine MRI Synthesis via Radial-Decomposed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09185)
*Shiyi Wang,Ruochen Sun,Xiang Li,Peirong Liu,Fangxu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseFlow3D：无需ECG，从单个ED三维体积生成完整4D心脏cine序列的生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准cine CMR采集需要ECG门控与反复屏气，而仅靠视觉逼真无法保证患者特异性的射血分数与心室容积准确性，因此需要一种无需ECG、能从单个体积生成准确4D序列的方法。

Method: 将心动周期表示为以ED与ES为锚点的分段线性相位；推理时用群体级模板提供相位。采用相位条件化rectified flow在隐空间生成运动轨迹，并用径向收缩分解将隐状态转成3D位移场（物理信息径向分量+图像条件残差），通过直接形变ED体积生成每帧，绕过VAE解码。

Result: 在ACDC与M&Ms联合基准上取得最低EF平均绝对误差、唯一正的左心室容积曲线R²及最佳分布质量；消融验证各组件贡献，下游任务（分割、病理分类、标签传播、心肌应变）显示合成序列与位移场的实用性。

Conclusion: PhaseFlow3D为无ECG条件下生成患者特异性4D心脏cine序列提供了可行方案，并支持多种下游临床分析任务。

Abstract: Cine cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) captures the cardiac cycle as a four-dimensional (4D) sequence, but standard acquisition requires electrocardiogram (ECG) gating and repeated breath holds. Visual realism alone does not establish accurate patient-specific ejection fraction (EF) or ventricular volumes. We present PhaseFlow3D, a generative framework that synthesizes a complete 4D cine sequence from a single end-diastolic (ED) three-dimensional (3D) volume without ECG. To capture asymmetric systolic and diastolic dynamics, it represents the cardiac cycle as a piecewise linear phase anchored at ED and end-systolic (ES) time points. At inference, a population-level canonical template supplies this phase without patient-specific temporal information. A phase-conditioned rectified flow model generates a cardiac motion trajectory in latent space. Radial Contraction Decomposition converts each latent state into a 3D displacement field, combining a physics-informed radial component for centripetal myocardial contraction with an image-conditioned residual for rotation and out-of-plane motion. Each frame is generated by directly warping the ED volume, bypassing variational autoencoder decoding. On the combined ACDC and M&Ms benchmark, PhaseFlow3D achieves the lowest EF mean absolute error, the only positive left-ventricular volume-curve $R^2$, and the best distributional quality among compared methods. Ablations confirm each component's contribution. Downstream evaluations demonstrate the utility of the synthesized sequences and displacement fields for segmentation, pathology classification, label propagation, and myocardial strain analysis.

</details>


### [23] [R-CNN-Based Chess Position Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09191)
*Paras Govind,Ognjen Arandjelović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于R-CNN的框架，从单张三维棋盘图像中识别棋局，显著提升棋子检测与棋盘几何估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭单张三维棋盘图像重建棋局需同时估计棋盘相对相机的位置与朝向、格子占用及棋子类型（含颜色），任务复杂且具有挑战性。

Method: 采用独立组件的R-CNN框架：棋子识别用改进Faster R-CNN（类加权目标、更深分类头），保留备选假设并利用棋子数量和格子占用约束优化；棋盘检测用Mask R-CNN关键点头预测八个标记边界关键点，提供冗余对应以估计单应性并编码朝向，将棋子框代表点映射到8x8网格。

Result: 合成数据集上，棋子检测mAP从61.59%提升至90.14%；97.11%预测棋盘关键点误差在图像对角线1%以内；使用真值棋子框与预测单应性可正确分配所有测试位置；完整框架精确恢复76.61%测试棋局，允许至多一个错误格子时达96.49%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效结合棋子识别与棋盘几何估计，在合成数据上实现高精度棋局重建，验证了组件独立设计与约束优化的有效性。

Abstract: Performing chess game position recognition solely from a single image of a three-dimensional board requires predicting the position and orientation of the board relative to the camera, the occupancy of squares and the piece type, which includes its colour. We propose an R-CNN-based framework with independent components for piece recognition and board geometry estimation, whose predictions are combined to reconstruct the position. For piece recognition, we adapt Faster R-CNN using a class-weighted objective and a deeper classification head. The detector operates directly on the input image, retaining alternative piece hypotheses that are subsequently refined using constraints on piece counts and square occupancy. For board detection, we introduce an octagonal arrangement of eight labelled boundary keypoints, predicted using the keypoint head of Mask R-CNN. These provide redundant correspondences for homography estimation and encode board orientation. The estimated homography maps representative points from the piece boxes to an 8x8 grid. On a synthetic dataset, the modifications to piece detection increase mean average precision from 61.59% to 90.14%. Of the predicted board keypoints, 97.11% are within 1% of the image diagonal of their labelled targets. Using ground-truth piece boxes with the predicted homographies gives correct square assignments for every test position. The complete framework recovers 76.61% of test positions exactly and 96.49% with at most one incorrect square.

</details>


### [24] [StableGrasp: Reconstructing Physically Stable Human Hand Grasps from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09195)
*Han Jiang,Etienne Vouga,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableGrasp 是一个基于可微仿真的优化框架，通过分离视觉手部姿态与决定抓取力的控制目标，从单张 RGB 图像重建物理稳定的抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张 RGB 图像重建物理稳定的人手抓取具有挑战性：物理建模本身困难，且问题不仅需要估计受视觉约束的手部姿态，还需估计能稳定抓取的控制目标。现有方法要么只建模视觉手部几何而忽略物理，要么依赖不够合理的物理建模，限制了所生成抓取的物理有效性。

Method: 方法将视觉手部姿态与决定抓取力的控制目标显式分离，提出可微仿真优化框架。在可微仿真器中联合优化手部几何与控制，通过最小化抓取的动能实现，同时对几何施加正则化以保持视觉一致性与几何合理性。

Result: 在严格物理仿真下，重建抓取的稳定性大幅提升，同时保持与输入图像的视觉一致性以及几何合理性。相比其他手部控制策略，本方法产生的抓取稳定得多，并能将纯视觉抓取重建流程的输出转化为物理稳定的抓取。

Conclusion: StableGrasp 通过物理仿真驱动的优化提升了抓取重建的物理有效性，可增强现有视觉抓取重建流程。

Abstract: Reconstructing a physically stable human grasp from a single RGB image is challenging because physically modeling grasps is itself difficult, and the problem requires estimating not only a visually constrained hand pose but also a control target that stabilizes the grasp. Existing methods either model only visual hand geometry without considering physics, or rely on less plausible physical modeling, which limits the physical validity of the resulting grasps. In this paper, we present StableGrasp, a differentiable simulation-based optimization framework that explicitly separates the visual hand pose from the control target that determines the grasping forces. Our method jointly optimizes hand geometry and control by minimizing the kinetic energy of the grasp in a differentiable simulator, while regularizing the hand geometry to preserve visual consistency and geometric plausibility. The reconstructed grasps are substantially more stable under rigorous physical simulation, while remaining visually consistent with the input images and geometrically plausible. Experiments show that our approach produces far more stable grasps than alternative hand-control strategies, benefiting visual-only grasp reconstruction pipelines by turning their outputs into physically stable grasps.

</details>


### [25] [StyleFields: Multi-Scale AdaIN-Modulated Implicit SDFs for Coarse-to-Fine 3D Shape Reconstruction and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09200)
*Ehsan Garaaghaji,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StyleFields，一种基于DeepSDF的高保真3D重建架构，通过深度感知调制实现可控的几何风格混合，无需部件标签或对抗训练，并应用于汽车空气动力学优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现可控的几何风格混合，使一个物体的粗略结构与另一个物体的精细细节相结合，同时避免依赖部件标签或对抗训练。

Method: 核心为深度感知调制：不用单一全局编码，而是在多个解码器深度通过多级自适应实例归一化（AdaIN）注入潜变量，并以粗到细的调度监督匹配的辅助头，同时逐步增长网络深度。这使浅层对齐全局形状、深层对齐高频细节，实现内容-风格解耦。

Result: 实现忠实重建与令人信服的跨实例混合；消融实验显示在注入深度和监督粒度上均有稳定增益。在汽车空气动力学应用中，用学习到的代理阻力预测器作为可微目标优化重建的汽车，通过冻结互补潜流实现全局形态或表面细节的定向编辑。

Conclusion: StyleFields为可控隐式重建及下游性能驱动设计提供了一种简单而有效的方案。

Abstract: We introduce StyleFields, a DeepSDF-based architecture for high-fidelity 3D reconstruction that enables controllable geometric style mixing: the coarse structure of one object can be combined with the fine-scale details of another. The core idea is depth-aware modulation: instead of a single global code, we inject latents via multi-level Adaptive Instance Normalization at several decoder depths, and supervise matching auxiliary heads with a coarse-to-fine schedule while gradually growing network depth. This aligns early layers with global shape and later layers with high-frequency detail, achieving content-style decoupling without part labels or adversarial training. StyleFields delivers faithful reconstructions, convincing cross-instance hybrids, and consistent gains in ablations over injection depth and supervision granularity. We further demonstrate a practical application in automotive aerodynamics: a learned surrogate drag predictor serves as a differentiable objective to optimize reconstructed cars, allowing targeted edits of global form or surface details by freezing the complementary latent stream. StyleFields offers a simple, effective recipe for controllable implicit reconstruction and downstream performance-driven design.

</details>


### [26] [Do Vision Models Learn Physical Constraints or Rendering Shortcuts? A Counterfactual Benchmark for Grounded Physical Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09205)
*M. Moein Esfahani,Sepehr Salem,Mohammed Alser,Vince Calhoun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出物理合理性诊断任务与反事实基准，用Mitsuba 3生成5,500张图像，检测编辑图像的物理违规类型并定位、解释；基线模型在标准集达64.0–67.8% macro-F1，但干预偏移下骤降至40.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像编辑模型虽能遵循文本指令，却常破坏场景物理（如物体无影、镜子不反射、物体悬浮）。现有工作缺乏对物理合理性的系统诊断，即判断是否违规、命名违规类型、定位区域并用语言解释。

Method: 构建反事实基准：用Mitsuba 3生成500个场景族共5,500张图像，每族含1张干净图和10张匹配违规图，覆盖阴影、反射、支撑、表面响应、遮挡五类。渲染管线提供类别标签、受影响区域掩码与框、场景元数据及解释目标。以LLaVA-1.5-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3.5-8B作为诊断基线（非提出方法）。

Result: 在1,650张合成测试集上，适配基线在标准留出场景达64.0–67.8%类别macro-F1；LLaVA-1.5-7B在干预偏移下从64.0%降至40.8%。

Conclusion: 该差距表明高同分布准确率部分依赖与渲染和反事实构建相关的线索，物理合理性诊断仍具挑战，模型泛化能力不足。

Abstract: Modern image editing models can satisfy a text instruction while breaking the physics of the edited scene. A new object may cast no shadow, a mirror may fail to reflect visible geometry, or an object may float above a surface that should support it. We study physical plausibility diagnosis, detecting whether an edited image violates scene physics, naming the violation type, localizing the affected region, and explaining the failure in language. We introduce a counterfactual benchmark whose controlled synthetic component uses Mitsuba~3 to generate 5,500 images from 500 scene families. Each family contains one clean image and ten matched violations involving shadows, reflection, support, surface response, and occlusion. The renderer pipeline provides category labels, affected-region masks and boxes, scene metadata, and explanation targets. We use LLaVA-1.5-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and InternVL3.5-8B as diagnostic baselines rather than proposed methods. On a 1,650-image synthetic test set, the adapted baselines reach 64.0--67.8\% category macro-F1 on standard held-out scenes. For LLaVA-1.5-7B, category macro-F1 falls from 64.0\% on the standard split to 40.8\% under intervention shift. This gap shows that high in-distribution accuracy partly reflects cues tied to rendering and counterfactual construction.

</details>


### [27] [A Deterministic Evidence Layer for Vision-Language Autism Screening from Naturalistic Home Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09217)
*Wenqi Li,Mindi Ruan,Chuanbo Hu,Shuo Wang,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文保留VLM冻结，将决策从模型中移出，通过"有依据的感知约束+确定性证据加权打分器"构建事件表并分层风险，在家庭自由玩耍视频上实现稳定且可解释的ASD早期识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD依赖专家观察诊断，专家资源是早期识别的瓶颈。VLM虽能描述儿童行为，但据其描述得出的判定不稳定：温度0下、同一主干八个管线配置中16-37%片段在重复运行间标签改变，根源在服务栈。因此需要把决策从模型内部移出，保证判定的可复现与可解释。

Method: 保持VLM冻结。在其上施加有依据的感知约束，由VLM写出带时间戳、术语表标签的事件表，记录诱发刺激与反面证据；再用纯文本阶段按年龄校准每行置信度；最后由确定性证据加权打分器汇总为证据总分并分层为风险类别，使每个决策可分解为具名的逐特征贡献，并能从保存的事件表重新打分。

Result: 在43段照护者录制、无固定流程的学龄前儿童家庭自由玩耍片段上，三次运行取得AUC 0.851±0.012、准确率86.0%、F1 71.8；74.4%片段在每次运行中均被正确标注（零样本基线为60.5%），且每次运行都未误报任一典型发育片段（零样本为31中的9段）。同主干消融表明增益来自经确定性打分器读取的有依据感知约束。

Conclusion: 将判定从VLM内部移至确定性的、可追溯的打分流程，可显著提升ASD视频筛查判定的稳定性、准确性与可解释性，并降低对典型发育儿童的误报，为专家资源受限的早期识别提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is diagnosed through specialist observation of a child's social behavior, and access to that expertise is the bottleneck for early identification. Vision-language models (VLMs) describe a child's behavior from video well; the verdict drawn from the description is unstable: at temperature~0, across eight pipeline configurations on one backbone, 16--37\% of clips change their predicted label between repeated runs, and the cause lies in the serving stack. We keep the VLM frozen and move the decision out of the model. Under our grounded perception constraints the VLM writes an event table of timestamped, glossary-labeled events that names the eliciting press and logs counter-evidence; a text-only stage age-calibrates the confidence of each row; a deterministic weight-of-evidence scorer sums it into an evidence total and stratifies it into a risk category, so every decision decomposes into named per-feature contributions and can be re-scored from the saved table. On 43 caregiver-recorded, protocol-free home free-play clips of preschool children, the pipeline reaches AUC $0.851 \pm 0.012$, 86.0\% accuracy, and F$_1$ 71.8 over three runs. It labels 74.4\% of clips correctly in every run (60.5\% for the zero-shot baseline) and flags no typically developing clip in every run (9 of 31 at zero-shot). An ablation on the same backbone attributes the gain to the grounded perception constraints read through the deterministic scorer.

</details>


### [28] [PVSync: A Unified Lip-Sync Expert for Timing and Articulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09223)
*Kevin Stephen,Varun Menon,Timo Mertens,Nikita Drobyshev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PVSync：统一音视频偏移估计与音素级发音评分模型，无需人工标注，在唇形同步质量排序上更贴近人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 唇动可以只匹配说话时序而不匹配实际语音内容，现有唇形同步评估指标（如LSE-C）难以捕捉这种不匹配，因此需要更能反映真实发音一致性的评估方法。

Method: 提出PVSync统一模型，结合窗口级对比学习进行同步估计，并引入音素级发音目标，对齐同一视素类别在不同片段中的音频与视频嵌入；视素标签由强制对齐转录自动生成，无需人工标注。

Result: 在13个说话头生成模型输出的偏移校正视频上，PVSync与人类唇形同步质量排序的相关性（Spearman 0.83）高于LSE-C（0.34）；在自动构建的留出语音基准上，区分视素匹配与不匹配音视频对的ROC AUC达0.91；在时间偏移恢复上优于SyncNet和MTD-VocaLiST。

Conclusion: PVSync能更准确地评估唇形同步与发音一致性，且可在无人工标注条件下自动构建基准，代码与数据将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Lip movements can match the timing of speech without matching the spoken sounds. We introduce PVSync, a unified model for audio-visual offset estimation and phoneme-level articulation scoring. PVSync combines window-level contrastive learning for synchronisation with a phoneme-level articulation objective that aligns audio and video embeddings of the same viseme class across clips. Visemes group phonemes with similar visible articulation. Viseme labels are derived automatically from forced-aligned transcripts, without manual annotations. On offset-corrected videos from 13 talking-head video generation models, PVSync matches human rankings of lip-sync quality more closely than LSE-C, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.83 versus 0.34. On an automatically constructed benchmark from held-out speech, PVSync distinguishes viseme-matched from mismatched audio-visual pairs with an ROC AUC of 0.91. PVSync also outperforms SyncNet and MTD-VocaLiST in temporal offset recovery on held-out in-the-wild clips. Code and benchmark data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [29] [Pooling Representation Autoencoders for Efficient Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09242)
*Ramón Calvo-González,Youssef Saied,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoolDINO 提出一种可学习的仿射池化算子，通过合并相邻 token 降低 RAE 的密集 token 网格带来的高生成成本，在保持两阶段流程的同时实现 4x/16x 压缩与显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAE 从预训练视觉特征生成图像，但其密集 token 网格使生成建模开销高昂。受局部特征相关性启发，作者希望在不引入额外特征自编码器的情况下降低 token 数量。

Method: 提出 PoolDINO：一个可学习的仿射池化算子，用于合并相邻 token。该算子与 RGB 解码器联合训练，从而保留标准两阶段 RAE 流程，无需单独的特征自编码器。在 ImageNet-256 上测试 4x 与 16x token 压缩。

Result: 在 4x token 压缩下，内部引导下生成质量相当；16x 压缩则以一定质量换取更高效率。固定 100 采样步数时，潜在采样吞吐分别提升 3.7x 与 9.0x。分类与密集预测评估显示，相当的引导生成质量可能伴随其他任务性能下降。

Conclusion: PoolDINO 可在保持 RAE 标准流程的同时显著提升生成效率，但压缩带来的效率提升与下游任务性能之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) generate images from pre-trained visual fea- tures, but their dense token grids make generative modeling expensive. Motivated by local feature correlations, we introduce PoolDINO, a learned affine pooling operator that merges neighboring tokens. Training the pooling operator jointly with the RGB decoder preserves the standard two-stage RAE procedure without a separate feature auto-encoder. On ImageNet-256, 4x token compression retains comparable generation quality under internal guidance, while 16x compression trades some quality for greater efficiency. At a fixed budget of 100 sampling steps, latent-sampling throughput increases by 3.7x and 9.0x, respectively, relative to the unpooled baseline. Classification and dense prediction evaluations show that comparable guided generation quality can coexist with weaker performance on other tasks.

</details>


### [30] [Adaptive Visual Token Reduction for Accelerated Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09252)
*Seyoung Jeong,Jong Pil Yun,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReFIT，一种指令引导的视觉 token 缩减框架，通过窗口重塑与 token 精炼，在降低计算成本的同时提升 VQA 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率、信息丰富的图像在 LVLM 中计算开销大，需要减少视觉 token。但现有方法多为单 token 剪枝或固定尺寸裁剪，难以保留如水平或垂直拉长文本这类具有空间结构的信息。

Method: ReFIT 包含两个模块：Relevance-Guided Window Reshaping（RWR）通过自适应目标区域的空问特征来捕获与指令相关的区域；Instruction-Guided Token Refinement（ITR）进一步去除不必要的视觉 token。

Result: 在四个 VQA 基准上，ReFIT 在提升答案准确率的同时降低了计算成本；定性结果显示了其在定位相关区域和去除冗余视觉信息方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ReFIT 通过指令引导的窗口重塑与 token 精炼，实现了高效且更准确的 LVLM 推理。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models achieve strong VQA performance, but processing high-resolution, information-rich images requires substantial computation, motivating visual token reduction. However, existing methods often prune individual tokens or rely on fixed-size cropping, limiting their ability to preserve spatially structured information such as horizontally or vertically elongated text. To address this limitation, we propose ReFIT, an instruction-guided visual token reduction framework for efficient LVLM inference. ReFIT consists of Relevance-Guided Window Reshaping (RWR) and Instruction-Guided Token Refinement (ITR), where RWR captures instruction-relevant regions by adapting to their spatial characteristics, while ITR further removes unnecessary visual tokens. Experiments on four VQA benchmarks demonstrate that ReFIT improves answer accuracy while reducing computational cost, and qualitative results demonstrate its effectiveness in localizing relevant regions and removing unnecessary visual information.

</details>


### [31] [PhyDiCT: Plug-and-Play CT Reconstruction from Sparse X-Rays via Differentiable Rendering and Strong Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09253)
*Weicheng Dai,Shantanu Ghosh,Kayhan Batmanghelich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyDiCT：无需训练，结合可微物理前向模型与文本条件扩散先验，通过Split Gibbs采样重建3D肺部CT，性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量X射线投影重建3D CT是高度病态的逆问题，因体积信息丢失严重，需要强先验与物理约束共同作用。

Method: 提出PhyDiCT免训练框架：基于Beer-Lambert定律的可微物理前向模型 + 文本条件扩散先验（不微调），用Split Gibbs采样联合优化投影保真度（奖励）与先验一致性，并引入测试时细化步骤提升真实感与解剖连贯性。

Result: 在公开3D CT数据集上以感知和语义指标评估，超越现有即插即用扩散方法与全训练重建方法，SSIM相比全训练方法提升约7.5%。

Conclusion: 将强生成先验与成像物理结合可显著提升重建质量，验证了物理引导扩散先验在稀疏视角CT重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D Computed Tomography (CT) images from a few X-ray projections is a highly ill-posed inverse problem due to the loss of volumetric information. We propose PhyDiCT, a training-free framework that integrates a differentiable Physics-based forward model, grounded in the Beer-Lambert law, with a text-conditioned Diffusion as a strong prior to reconstruct 3D lung CT images. We refer to our approach as training-free since the prior model is used without fine-tuning, and our goal is to steer the denoising procedure to generate samples consistent with X-ray observations. We guide the diffusion generation using Split Gibbs sampling to jointly optimize for projection fidelity (reward) and consistency with prior knowledge. Also, we introduce a test-time refinement step that enhances image realism and anatomical coherence. We extensively evaluate our method on publicly available 3D CT datasets using both perceptual and semantic metrics, demonstrating that it surpasses existing plug-and-play diffusion and fully trained reconstruction approaches. Our findings highlight that combining a strong generative prior with the underlying physics of image formation substantially improves reconstruction quality, e.g., 7.5\% improvement on SSIM compared to full training methods. Code will be released at https://github.com/batmanlab/PhyDiCT.

</details>


### [32] [Hardware-aware Calibrated Clustered Attention for Efficient Visual Geometric Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09274)
*Weitian Wang,Shubham Rai,Cecilia De La Parra,Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分块聚类注意力（BC attention）加速 VGGT 的全局注意力层，通过块内聚类降低开销与数据搬运，并引入哈希超平面校准与阈值误差补偿减少聚类误差，在 GPU 上实现显著加速且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 是首个能在单次前向中联合推断相机位姿、深度与稠密几何的 3D 场景重建模型，但其联合推断机制依赖超长序列的全局注意力层，导致严重的延迟瓶颈。现有聚类注意力机制存在聚类误差这一瓶颈，且查询聚类与片上/片外存储间数据搬运开销大，难以扩展到长序列。

Method: 提出分块聚类注意力（BC attention）：将聚类限制在 HW 友好的邻域块内，降低查询聚类的计算开销以及片上与片外存储间昂贵的数据搬运，使其可扩展到长序列并在 GPU 上获得实际延迟改善。同时引入哈希超平面校准方法与基于阈值的误差补偿方法，以高效降低聚类误差。

Result: GPU 实验表明，校准后的 BC attention 将全局注意力层加速 2.10–2.63 倍，整个骨干网络加速 1.77–2.35 倍，大场景下精度损失可忽略（1%）。在允许较小性能损失（< 5%）时，校准 BC attention 进一步在全局注意力层取得 2.26–2.87 倍、在骨干网络取得 1.90–2.55 倍的延迟改善。

Conclusion: BC attention 通过块内聚类与校准/补偿机制，有效缓解了 VGGT 全局注意力层的延迟瓶颈，在保持重建精度的同时实现了显著的 GPU 推理加速。

Abstract: The Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) marks a significant leap forward in 3D scene reconstruction, as it is the first model that directly infers all key 3D attributes (camera poses, depths, and dense geometry) jointly in one pass. However, this joint inference mechanism requires global attention layers with extremely long sequences that causes a significant latency bottleneck. In this paper, we propose blockwise clustered attention (BC attention) to accelerate the global attention layers in VGGT. By limiting the clustering within HW-friendly neighborhood blocks, BC attention reduces the computation overhead of query clustering as well as the costly data movement between on- and off-chip memory. This enables BC attention to scale to long sequences and deliver practical latency improvements on GPUs. Moreover, we introduce a hashing hyperplane calibration method and a threshold-based error compensation method to reduce clustering errors efficiently, which is a bottleneck in the current clustered attention mechanism. Overall, our experiments on GPU demonstrate that calibrated BC attention accelerates the global attention layers by 2.10-2.63$\times$ and the whole backbone by 1.77-2.35$\times$ with negligible loss (1%) for large scenes. With a small performance loss (< 5%), calibrated BC attention further achieves a 2.26-2.87$\times$ latency improvement on the global attention layers and a 1.90-2.55$\times$ improvement on the backbone.

</details>


### [33] [EM-SNN: Efficiently Modulated Spiking Neural Network for Remote Sensing Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09275)
*Jie Shao,Jiaqi Ma,Wenwen Min,Beihang Song,Ning Chen,Youfa Liu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EM-SNN脉冲神经网络框架用于遥感图像去雾，通过TM-LIF神经元和SSM模块缓解雾导致的高频衰减与脉冲阈值耦合问题，在四个数据集上以四分之一能耗取得竞争性去雾性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽比人工神经网络更节能，但在遥感图像去雾领域应用有限。核心难题在于雾引起的高频衰减与离散脉冲阈值化之间的耦合：这种交互抑制了弱响应，从根本上限制了脉冲去雾模型对边缘、纹理和细节的恢复。

Method: 提出EM-SNN（高效调制脉冲神经网络）专用框架。包含两部分：1）统计驱动的阈值调制LIF神经元（TM-LIF），自适应补偿雾导致的对比度压缩；2）脉冲Sobel调制模块（SSM），增强结构线索并减少脉冲特征传播中的深度衰减。通过联合调制激活尺度与结构表示，在提升去雾性能的同时保持SNN的事件驱动稀疏性。

Result: 在HRSD、RICE、RRSHID和SateHaze1K四个数据集上的实验表明，EM-SNN取得具有竞争力的去雾性能，同时能耗仅为强ANN基线SFRDP-Net的四分之一。

Conclusion: EM-SNN通过阈值与结构双重调制，有效缓解了雾衰减与脉冲阈值的耦合问题，在保持SNN稀疏低能耗优势的前提下实现了高质量遥感图像去雾。

Abstract: Although spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide an energy-efficient alternative to artificial neural networks (ANNs), their application to remote sensing image dehazing remains limited. A key challenge arises from the coupling between haze-induced high-frequency attenuation and discrete spike thresholding. This interaction suppresses weak responses and fundamentally limits the recovery of edges, textures, and fine details in spiking dehazing models. To address this challenge, we propose the Efficiently Modulated Spiking Neural Network (EM-SNN), a dedicated spiking framework tailored to remote sensing image dehazing. EM-SNN integrates a statistics-driven Threshold-Modulated Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (TM-LIF) neuron to adaptively compensate for haze-induced contrast compression, together with a Spike Sobel Modulation (SSM) module that enhances structural cues and reduces depth-wise attenuation during spiking feature propagation. By jointly modulating activation scales and structural representations, EM-SNN improves dehazing performance while preserving the inherent event-driven sparsity of SNNs. Experiments on HRSD, RICE, RRSHID, and SateHaze1K demonstrate that EM-SNN achieves competitive dehazing performance while consuming only one quarter of the energy of the strong ANN baseline SFRDP-Net.

</details>


### [34] [Why VLMs Miss Small Objects, and When Zooming In Is Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09313)
*Junzhe Shi,Yuan Gan,Shida Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关于视觉语言模型（VLM）处理大图中微小物体的理论，基于视觉令牌数S与单次调用覆盖内容L两个量，揭示缩放成本与识别-成本前沿，论证局部拆解（decomposition）仍具前景，并用约17.7万次请求在797张图像上验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在大图中常漏掉小物体。作者希望回答三个问题：限制VLM处理小物体的因素是什么、其中哪些限制能通过更强的模型消除、经典的局部拆解方法是否仍有未来。

Method: 构建基于图像接口两个量（S，物体边长上的视觉令牌数；L，单次调用需覆盖的内容）的理论：推导整体图像在N令牌下的物体令牌上限，给出识别所需的令牌代价与recall-cost前沿；并分析局部拆解（views重叠一个物体、不相对整体缩小）条件下recall不下降的充分条件与成本上界。随后在约17.7万次请求、797张图像上做验证，包括受控图像、图纸、平面图、自然与合成图像。

Result: 整体发送W×H图像时，边长m物体的每边令牌数至多m*sqrt(N/(WH))，令牌预算翻倍仅使最大S提升41%；识别所需S的调用成本至少为S^2量级。在受控图像上，8个VLM的40个预测排序无一被违背；事后检验中89个隐含排序有61个显著成立、4个失败（均来自OpenAI模型在超出默认路径的像素数时）。在施工图上，所用规则从未显著降低recall，最多提升0.28。

Conclusion: 更强模型能改变物体所需S与单次调用承载量，但覆盖成本依旧存在。局部拆解仍有效：在不缩小且视图重叠一个物体的条件下不会降低recall，且成本有界、可被简单规则逼近。理论与实验共同支持分解式方法在大图小物体识别中的前景。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often miss small objects in large images. We ask three questions: what limits them, which of these limits better models can remove, and whether the classical way of handling large images, local decomposition, still has a future. We answer them with a theory built on two quantities of the image interface: S, the number of visual tokens across an object's side, and L, the content a call must cover. The limits: a W x H image sent whole within N tokens gives an object of side m at most m*sqrt(N/(WH)) tokens per side. Doubling the token budget N therefore raises the largest S a whole image can reach by only 41%, and seeing an image at the S an object needs costs at least order S^2 tokens whatever the model. If recognition improves gradually with S, any search strategy, zoom agents included, obeys a recall-cost frontier. What better models can change: the S an object needs and how much one call can carry, measured in bits per object found; the coverage cost remains. Decomposition: yes. Assuming only that more tokens per object and less content per call do not hurt on average, splitting an image cannot lower recall if no view zooms out relative to the whole image and views overlap by one object. Neither part of this condition can be dropped; we bound the cost of every such decomposition, and a simple rule approaches the bound as the image grows. We test the theory in about 177,000 requests on 797 images. On controlled images, none of 40 orderings predicted for 8 VLMs is violated. Checked after the fact on drawings, floor plans, natural and synthetic images, 61 of 89 implied orderings hold significantly and 4 fail, all for OpenAI models given more pixels than their default path. On construction drawings the rule never significantly lowered recall relative to the whole image and raised it by up to 0.28. Code and data: https://github.com/shijunzhe/vlm-small-objects

</details>


### [35] [SAREO-FM: Decoupled Semantic Supervision for SAR-EO Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09317)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAREO-FM框架，用分离的模态token与语义查询流，在共享编码器中同时实现掩码重建与语义对齐，在SAR-1M语料上预训练，兼顾单模态迁移与SAR-EO联合观测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与EO影像提供互补观测（SAR全天候昼夜成像，EO提供丰富外观与细粒度语义）。现有方法常让单一token流同时承担两种角色，造成角色冲突，难以兼顾传感器特性保留与语义理解。

Method: 避免单一token流承担双重角色：模态token通过掩码重建保留各传感器对场景的观测方式；可学习的语义查询在预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）指导下捕获场景语义内容。将查询与SAR、EO token联合编码于共享编码器，使查询获得模态相关的语义上下文，而模态token输出仍是掩码重建的显式目标，从而在共享编码器内分离语义与重建监督并保留交互。基于百万级SAR-1M语料预训练。

Result: 在SAR-only与EO-only输入上均实现较强的单模态迁移性能；在受益于互补感知的任务上，SAR-EO联合观测带来显著增益。

Conclusion: 通过分离语义与重建的token流并在共享编码器中交互，SAREO-FM有效统一了SAR与EO的互补表征学习，兼顾单模态与多模态场景。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) and electro-optical (EO) imagery provide complementary observations: SAR enables day-and-night, weather-resilient sensing, whereas EO provides rich appearance and fine-grained semantic cues. We introduce SAREO-FM, which avoids forcing a single token stream to serve two distinct roles: modality tokens preserve how each sensor observes the scene through masked reconstruction, while learnable semantic queries capture what the scene contains under guidance from a pretrained vision foundation model (VFM). By jointly encoding these queries with SAR and EO tokens, the queries acquire modality-grounded semantic context, while the modality-token outputs remain the explicit targets of masked reconstruction. This design assigns semantic and reconstruction supervision to separate token streams while preserving their interaction within the shared encoder. Pretrained on the million-scale SAR-1M corpus, SAREO-FM achieves strong unimodal transfer for both SAR-only and EO-only inputs, while delivering substantial gains from joint SAR--EO observations on tasks that benefit from complementary sensing.

</details>


### [36] [VIS-Ground: Video Interactive Storytelling with Contextual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09326)
*Bingxuan Li,Yiwen Song,Xueqing Wu,Yanzhou Pan,Yang Li,Kuang Su,Jingyun Liu,Sebastian Ko,Huan Zhang,Tong Zhang,Nanyun Peng,Tomas Pfister,Yale Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIS-Ground，将交互式视频续写中的"上下文接地"形式化为把异构输入转换为可执行约束模型，并通过结构化上下文抽象、生成约束归纳与约束视频生成三步实现，在三种视频生成骨干上平均绝对提升10.3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当允许观众在生成过程中介入时，观众请求可能对"接地的来源"和"当前已渲染的视频状态"存在潜在依赖，而这些依赖不会显式出现在任一单独输入中，只有在联合考虑来源、渲染历史与新意图时才浮现。现有交互式视频生成系统侧重遵循观众指令，而来源接地方法侧重与外部叙事/知识源对齐，二者都未回答一个根本问题：交互续写时模型应以什么上下文为接地依据，以及如何把异构、非结构化输入转化为该接地上下文。

Method: 本文将上下文接地定义为：将异构输入上下文转化为视频生成的可执行约束模型。为此提出VIS-Ground，包含三个环节：(1) Structured Context Abstraction——恢复接地状态与跨上下文依赖；(2) Generation Constraints Induction——把相关依赖投影为候选特定的约束；(3) Constrained Video Generation——通过规划、验证、修订与渲染来强制执行这些约束。

Result: 在三种视频生成骨干模型上，VIS-Ground均取得最高的总体综合得分，相较各骨干对应的最强基线平均绝对提升10.3分。细粒度分析显示，在叙事接地与知识接地两类场景中均有增益，同时揭示了在依赖提取以及视频渲染阶段的忠实实现方面仍存在的挑战。

Conclusion: 交互式视频续写的关键在于明确并构造生成所依据的接地上下文。将异构输入转化为可执行约束模型是一条有效路径，VIS-Ground在多骨干上验证了其一致优势；未来工作需聚焦依赖提取的准确性与渲染阶段的忠实落地。

Abstract: Video interactive storytelling enables viewers to actively steer how a video unfolds. However, once we allow viewers to intervene during generation, a new challenge arises: The viewer's request can have latent dependencies on both the grounding source and the current rendered video state. These dependencies may not be explicitly stated in any individual input, but emerge only when the source, rendered history, and new viewer intent are considered jointly. Existing interactive video generation systems primarily emphasize following viewer instructions, while source-grounded video generation methods focus on aligning generated content with an external narrative or knowledge source. This leaves a fundamental question underexplored: What context should a generation model ground on during interactive continuation, and how can heterogeneous, unstructured inputs be transformed into such grounding context? In this work, we formulate contextual grounding as the process of transforming heterogeneous input context into an executable constraint model for video generation.
